## 公告系统

> 来源: 通用功能 (id:20409)

### 示例 1

```lua
-- 仅在服务端调用
local PF = UGCAnnouncementSystem.GetLatestAnnouncements()

PF:Then(function(PromiseFuture)
    local Announcements = PromiseFuture:Get()
    for i, Announcement in ipairs(Announcements) do
        ugcprint(string.format("[公告] %s - %s (置顶: %s)",
            Announcement.Title,
            Announcement.Content,
            tostring(Announcement.bTop)))
    end
end)

PF:Else(function(PromiseFuture)
    ugcprint("[UGCAnnouncementSystem.GetLatestAnnouncements] Failed, timeout")
end)
```

### 示例 2

```lua
local AnnouncementManager = {}

function AnnouncementManager:RefreshAnnouncements()
    -- 需要先判断是否在服务端
    if not UGCGameSystem.IsServer() then
        return
    end

    local PF = UGCAnnouncementSystem.GetLatestAnnouncements()

    PF:Then(function(PromiseFuture)
        local Announcements = PromiseFuture:Get()
        -- 在这里处理公告数据，例如筛选置顶公告
        for _, Announcement in ipairs(Announcements) do
            if Announcement.bTop then
                -- 处理置顶公告，比如同步给客户端展示
                ugcprint("[公告-置顶] " .. Announcement.Title)
            end
        end
    end)

    PF:Else(function(PromiseFuture)
        ugcprint("[AnnouncementManager] 获取公告失败，请检查网络状态")
    end)
end

return AnnouncementManager
```

```lua
-- 购买商品后的结果，通过UGCCommoditySystem.BuyUGCCommodityResultDelegate返回，可以在GameState中增加和移除委托监听
	function UGCGameState:ReceiveBeginPlay()
			UGCGameState.SuperClass.ReceiveBeginPlay(self)
			UGCCommoditySystem.BuyUGCCommodityResultDelegate:Add(self.OnBuyUGCCommodityResult, self)
	end

	function UGCGameState:ReceiveEndPlay()
			UGCGameState.SuperClass.ReceiveEndPlay(self)
			UGCCommoditySystem.BuyUGCCommodityResultDelegate:Remove(self.OnBuyUGCCommodityResult, self)
	end

	--可以通过参数得知玩家物品的变化已经是否购买成功
	function UGCGameState:OnBuyUGCCommodityResult(bSucceeded, PlayerKey, CommodityID, Count, UID, ProductID)
			local Result = string.format(
					"(UGCGameState:OnBuyUGCCommodityResult bSucceeded=%s PlayerKey=%s CommodityID=%s Count=%s)",
					tostring(bSucceeded),
					tostring(PlayerKey),
					tostring(CommodityID),
					tostring(Count),
					tostring(ProductID)
			)
			ugcprint(Result)

			local cond = UGCGameSystem.IsServer()
			if cond then
					local Result = UGCCommoditySystem.GetAllPlayerUGCCommodityList()
					local PlayerController = UGCGameSystem.GetPlayerControllerByPlayerKey(PlayerKey)
					ugcprint(ToTreeString(Result[PlayerController:GetInt64UID()]))
			else
					local Result = UGCCommoditySystem.GetUGCCommodityList()
					ugcprint(ToTreeString(Result))
			end
	end
```

```lua
	function UGCGameState:ReceiveBeginPlay()
			UGCGameState.SuperClass.ReceiveBeginPlay(self)
			UGCCommoditySystem.UseUGCCommodityResultDelegate:Add(self.OnUseUGCCommodityResult, self)
	end

	function UGCGameState:ReceiveEndPlay()
			UGCGameState.SuperClass.ReceiveEndPlay(self)
			UGCCommoditySystem.UseUGCCommodityResultDelegate:Remove(self.OnUseUGCCommodityResult, self)
	end

	-- 可以通过参数得知玩家物品的变化已经是否使用成功
	function UGCGameState:OnUseUGCCommodityResult(bSucceeded, PlayerKey, CommodityID, Count, UID)
			ugcprint("UGCGameState:OnUseUGCCommodityResult")

			local Result = string.format(
					"(UGCGameState:OnUseUGCCommodityResult bSucceeded=%s PlayerKey=%s CommodityID=%s Count=%s)",
					tostring(bSucceeded),
					tostring(PlayerKey),
					tostring(CommodityID),
					tostring(Count),
					tostring(UID)
			)
			ugcprint(Result)

			local cond = UGCGameSystem.IsServer()
			if cond then
					local Result = UGCCommoditySystem.GetAllPlayerUGCCommodityList()
					local PlayerController = UGCGameSystem.GetPlayerControllerByPlayerKey(PlayerKey)
					ugcprint(ToTreeString(Result[PlayerController:GetInt64UID()]))
			else
					local Result = UGCCommoditySystem.GetUGCCommodityList()
					ugcprint(ToTreeString(Result))
			end
	end
```

```lua
	local OwnedItems = GamePartActor:GetOwnedVirtualItems()
  log_tree(OwnedItems)
end
```

## 结算模板

> 来源: 商业化及功能模板 (id:20232)

### 示例 1

```lua
--Main.lua部分代码

--UI控件的初始化函数
function MainUI:Construct()
	--给按键绑定点击事件回调
	self.ButtonBeginSettlement.OnClicked:Add(self.ButtonBeginSettlementOnClicked, self);
	self.ButtonEndSettlement.OnClicked:Add(self.ButtonEndSettlementOnClicked, self);
end

function MainUI:ButtonBeginSettlementOnClicked()
	--获取PlayerController
	local PlayerController = UGCGameSystem.GetLocalPlayerController();
	--调用PlayerController的ServerRPC函数
	UnrealNetwork.CallUnrealRPC(PlayerController, PlayerController, "BeginSettlement");
	return nil;
end

function MainUI:ButtonEndSettlementOnClicked()
	--获取PlayerController
	local PlayerController = UGCGameSystem.GetLocalPlayerController();
	--调用PlayerController的ServerRPC函数
	UnrealNetwork.CallUnrealRPC(PlayerController, PlayerController, "EndSettlement");
	return nil;
end

--UGCPlayerController.lua部分代码

--接收RPC调用
function UGCPlayerController:GetAvailableServerRPCs()
	return
  "BeginSettlement",
  "EndSettlement"
end

function UGCPlayerController:BeginSettlement()
	--获取所有的PlayerController
  local AllPlayerController = UGCGameSystem.GetAllPlayerController();

  --构造玩家结算数据
  local PlayerResults = {
		--第一位玩家的数据
		{
			PlayerKey = UGCGameSystem.GetPlayerKeyByPlayerController(AllPlayerController[1]);
			Results = {
				Kill = "35",
        Death = "5",
        Assist = "0",
        Time = "1分30秒",
        Val5 = "123",
        Val6 = "321",
        Val7 = "132",
        Val8 = "923",
        Val9 = "292"
			}
		},
    --第二位玩家的数据
		{
			PlayerKey = UGCGameSystem.GetPlayerKeyByPlayerController(AllPlayerController[2]);
			Results = {
				Kill = "25",
        Death = "20",
        Assist = "0",
        Time = "1分20秒",
        Val5 = "123",
        Val6 = "321",
        Val7 = "132",
        Val8 = "923",
        Val9 = "292"
			}
		}
	};

	--构造队伍结算数据
  local TeamResults = {
		--第一位玩家所在的队伍为胜利方
    {
    	TeamID = UGCPlayerControllerSystem.GetTeamID(AllPlayerController[1]);
      DetermingResult = "40";
    },
    --第二位玩家所在的队伍为失败方
    {
    	TeamID = UGCPlayerControllerSystem.GetTeamID(AllPlayerController[2]);
      DetermingResult = "30";
    }
	};

	--开始结算
  GameResultManager:BeginSettlement(PlayerResults,TeamResults,false);
end

function UGCPlayerController:EndSettlement()
	--结束结算
  GameResultManager:EndSettlement();
end
```

### 示例 2

```lua
-- 生效范围：服务器
---@param PlayerResults table @玩家结算数据
---@param TeamResults table @队伍结算数据
---@param bDraw bool @是否平局
function GameResultManager:BeginSettlement(PlayerResults, TeamResults, bDraw)
end
```

### 示例 3

```lua

end
```


```lua
function UGCGameState:ReceiveBeginPlay()
    self.SuperClass.ReceiveBeginPlay(self);
    if self:HasAuthority() == true then

    else
        local TaskBtnClass = UGCObjectUtility.LoadClass(UGCGameSystem.GetUGCResourcesFullPath("ExtendResource/TaskTemplate/OfficialPackage/Asset/Task/Blueprint/WBP_TaskMainUIButton.WBP_TaskMainUIButton_C"));
        local TaskBtn = UGCWidgetManagerSystem.CreateWidget(TaskBtnClass);
        TaskBtn:AddToViewport();
    end
end
```

### 示例 5

```lua
-- 在 Server 或 Client 端注册监听（建议在玩家初始化时注册）
function MySystem:InitializeCompensateListener()
    -- 监听单笔补偿
    UGCCommoditySystem.CompensateUGCCommodityDelegate:Add(self.OnCompensateUGCCommodity, self)
end

---单笔补偿回调
---@param PlayerKey number
---@param UID number
---@param CommodityID number
---@param Count number
---@param ProductID number
function MySystem:OnCompensateUGCCommodity(PlayerKey, UID, CommodityID, Count, ProductID)
    print_dev(StringFormat_Dev("[MySystem] 收到单笔补偿 UID=%d CommodityID=%d Count=%d ProductID=%d",
        UID, CommodityID, Count, ProductID))

    -- 示例：记录补偿日志
    self:LogCompensation(PlayerKey, UID, CommodityID, Count, ProductID)

    -- 示例：客户端弹提示（仅客户端需要）
    if not UGCGameSystem.IsServer() then
        self:ShowCompensationTip(CommodityID, Count)
    end
end
```

### 示例 6

```lua
-- 仅在服务器端注册
function MySystem:InitializeBatchCompensateListener()
    if not UGCGameSystem.IsServer() then
        return
    end

    -- 监听批量补偿
    UGCCommoditySystem.CompensateUGCCommodityBatchDelegate:Add(self.OnCompensateUGCCommodityBatch, self)
end

---批量补偿回调
---@param PlayerKey number
---@param UID number
---@param CommodityList table
function MySystem:OnCompensateUGCCommodityBatch(PlayerKey, UID, CommodityList)
    print_dev(StringFormat_Dev("[MySystem] 收到批量补偿 UID=%d 商品数量=%d",
        UID, #CommodityList))

    -- 遍历补偿列表处理每一项
    for _, item in ipairs(CommodityList) do
        print_dev(StringFormat_Dev("[MySystem] 补偿项 CommodityID=%d Count=%d ProductID=%d",
            item.CommodityID, item.Count, item.ProductID))

        -- 示例：写入数据库或运营日志
        self:RecordCompensationToDB(UID, item.CommodityID, item.Count, item.ProductID)
    end

    -- 示例：发送运营事件上报
    self:ReportCompensationEvent(UID, CommodityList)
end
```

```lua
function MySystem:FinalizeCompensateListener()
    -- 移除单笔补偿监听
    UGCCommoditySystem.CompensateUGCCommodityDelegate:Remove(self.OnCompensateUGCCommodity, self)

    -- 移除批量补偿监听（如果在服务器注册了）
    if UGCGameSystem.IsServer() then
        UGCCommoditySystem.CompensateUGCCommodityBatchDelegate:Remove(self.OnCompensateUGCCommodityBatch, self)
    end
end

function MyPlayerController:ReceiveEndPlay()
    if UGCGameSystem.IsServer() then
        UGCCommoditySystem.BuyUGCCommodityResultBetweenGamesDelegate:Remove(self.OnBuyUGCCommodityResultBetweenGames, self)
    end
end
```

### 示例 9

```lua
-- ===== 注册（PlayerController:ReceiveBeginPlay，同步执行，勿延迟）=====
-- ⚠️ 真实行为提醒：发起一次批量补偿时，Batch 委托一次性广播整个 CommodityList，
--     同时单笔委托对列表中每件商品逐个广播 N 次（两个委托并存触发）。
--     推荐按需只监听其中之一，可彻底规避并存触发的防重复杂度。
function UGCPlayerController:ReceiveBeginPlay()
    -- ① 单笔委托：双端注册（客户端用于展示提示，服务端用于发奖）
    UGCCommoditySystem.CompensateUGCCommodityDelegate:Add(self.OnCompensateUGCCommodity, self)

    if UGCGameSystem.IsServer() then
        -- ② 批量委托：仅服务器注册；如同时监听单笔委托，须自己做精确防重（见 §B.2）
        -- UGCCommoditySystem.CompensateUGCCommodityBatchDelegate:Add(self.OnCompensateUGCCommodityBatch, self)
        -- ③ 跨局委托：仅服务器注册
        UGCCommoditySystem.BuyUGCCommodityResultBetweenGamesDelegate:Add(self.OnBuyUGCCommodityResultBetweenGames, self)
    end
end

-- ===== 单笔补偿回调（服务端发奖 + 落盘；客户端弹提示）=====
---@param PlayerKey number
---@param UID number
---@param CommodityID number
---@param Count number
---@param ProductID number
function UGCPlayerController:OnCompensateUGCCommodity(PlayerKey, UID, CommodityID, Count, ProductID)
    -- ① UID 首行过滤
    if UID ~= UGCGameSystem.GetUIDByPlayerController(self) then
        return
    end

    if UGCGameSystem.IsServer() then
        -- ② 商品→物品映射，映射不到打错误日志并安全跳过
        local ItemId = self:GetMappedRealItemID(CommodityID)
        if ItemId == nil or ItemId <= 0 then
            -- 记录错误日志（含 CommodityID/Count），便于定位
            return
        end

        -- ③ 按项目方案分支：手动方案 vs 自动方案（见 §A.1 要素 3 与上命名差异表）
        if self:IsAutoTransferEnabled() then
            -- ──────── 自动方案 ────────
            -- 项目已配置"商业化→背包自动转移"：不手动入包，改监听"补发/增加物品通知"
            -- 与"自动转移结果"事件，由系统自动把商业化存档物品转入背包。
            -- 此分支仅做打点 + 边界处理（监听补发通知用于背包满等异常场景感知）。
            self:WatchAutoTransferResult(CommodityID, Count)
        else
            -- ──────── 手动方案 ────────
            -- 关键顺序：先扣商业化存档 → 再入背包 → 最后显式落盘。
            -- 跳过任一步都会导致双账不一致（玩家拿双份 或 背包到账商业化存档不变）。
            -- API 站标准入口：UGCCommoditySystem.UseUGCCommodity2
            --   签名：(PlayerController, ObjectID, Icon, Desc, Count, bShowDialog)
            --   注意 Icon/Desc 可传 nil，会自动从 UGCObject 表读取；bShowDialog 在 DS 端忽略
            UGCCommoditySystem.UseUGCCommodity2(self, CommodityID, nil, nil, Count, false)

            -- 统一入包（使用增加道具的发放接口，绝不调用消费玩家已有道具的开包类接口）
            UGCBackpackSystemV2.AddItemV2(self, ItemId, Count)
        end

        -- ④ 显式落盘（不能用定时器 / 退出流程兜底；结算之后调用无效）
        UGCPlayerStateSystem.SavePlayerArchiveData(UID, self:BuildArchiveSnapshot())
    else
        -- 客户端：仅做展示，不重复发奖
        self:ShowCompensationTip(CommodityID, Count, ProductID)
    end
end

-- ===== 批量补偿回调（仅服务器；按需注册其一，见注册代码注释）=====
---@param PlayerKey number
---@param UID number
---@param CommodityList table  -- 兼容命名表与位置数组两种形态
function UGCPlayerController:OnCompensateUGCCommodityBatch(PlayerKey, UID, CommodityList)
    -- ① 服务端权限校验
    if not UGCGameSystem.IsServer() then
        return
    end
    -- ② UID 首行过滤
    if UID ~= UGCGameSystem.GetUIDByPlayerController(self) then
        return
    end

    local bAuto = self:IsAutoTransferEnabled()
    local archiveDirty = false

    for _, item in ipairs(CommodityList) do
        local CommodityID = item.CommodityID or item[1]
        local Count       = item.Count       or item[2]
        local ProductID   = item.ProductID   or item[3]
        if CommodityID and Count then
            if bAuto then
                -- 自动方案：仅打点 + 监听补发通知；不手动入包
                self:WatchAutoTransferResult(CommodityID, Count)
            else
                -- 手动方案：先扣商业化存档 → 再入背包
                UGCCommoditySystem.UseUGCCommodity2(self, CommodityID, nil, nil, Count, false)
                local ItemId = self:GetMappedRealItemID(CommodityID)
                if ItemId and ItemId > 0 then
                    UGCBackpackSystemV2.AddItemV2(self, ItemId, Count)
                end
            end
            archiveDirty = true
        end
    end

    -- ③ 批量补偿统一落盘（仅在确实修改过存档时落盘，避免无谓 IO）
    if archiveDirty then
        UGCPlayerStateSystem.SavePlayerArchiveData(UID, self:BuildArchiveSnapshot())
    end
end

-- ===== 跨局补偿回调：未就绪必须缓存，就绪后补发 =====
---@param PlayerKey number
---@param UID number
---@param CommodityID number
---@param Count number
function UGCPlayerController:OnBuyUGCCommodityResultBetweenGames(PlayerKey, UID, CommodityID, Count)
    if UID ~= UGCGameSystem.GetUIDByPlayerController(self) then
        return
    end

    -- 项目自定义：VIM = Virtual Item Manager；nil 即未实例化
    local VIM = self:GetVirtualItemManager()
    if VIM == nil then
        -- 未就绪 → 进缓存队列，等 GamePart 加载完成事件后补发
        table.insert(self.PendingCompensateList, {CommodityID = CommodityID, Count = Count})
        return
    end

    self:ApplyCompensateCommodity(CommodityID, Count)
end

-- ===== 注销（PlayerController:ReceiveEndPlay，对称移除）=====
function UGCPlayerController:ReceiveEndPlay()
    UGCCommoditySystem.CompensateUGCCommodityDelegate:Remove(self.OnCompensateUGCCommodity, self)

    if UGCGameSystem.IsServer() then
        -- 按需：若启用了批量委托则同步注销
        -- UGCCommoditySystem.CompensateUGCCommodityBatchDelegate:Remove(self.OnCompensateUGCCommodityBatch, self)
        UGCCommoditySystem.BuyUGCCommodityResultBetweenGamesDelegate:Remove(self.OnBuyUGCCommodityResultBetweenGames, self)
    end
end
```

```lua
local PlayerListGlobalActor = UGCGamePartSystem.PlayerListManager.GetGlobalActor()
if PlayerListGlobalActor then
    PlayerListGlobalActor.PlayerListUpdateDelegate:Add(function(PlayerListData)
        -- PlayerListData 已排序，可直接渲染UI
        for _, Entry in ipairs(PlayerListData) do
            print(Entry.PlayerName, Entry.SortValue, Entry.DisplayValue)
        end
    end)
end
```

```lua
-- 假设 TeammateList 是UI中队伍列表UI的变量名
local listWidget = self.TeammateList

function listWidget:GetCustomAttributeValue1(playerKey)
    local ps = UGCGameSystem.GetPlayerStateByPlayerKey(playerKey)
    if ps and UE.IsValid(ps) then
        -- 从 GameAttribute 系统读取击杀数
        return UGCAttributeSystem.GetGameAttributeValue(ps, "KillCount") or 0
    end
    return 0
end

function listWidget:GetCustomAttributeValue2(playerKey)
    local ps = UGCGameSystem.GetPlayerStateByPlayerKey(playerKey)
    if ps and UE.IsValid(ps) then
        return UGCAttributeSystem.GetGameAttributeValue(ps, "Score") or 0
    end
    return 0
end
```

## 关卡管理

> 来源: 玩法案例及模板/多人PVE模板 (id:20196)

### 示例 1

```lua
-- UGCLevelReward.lua

local UGCLevelReward = {}

function UGCLevelReward:LuaExecute()
	-- 获取当前关卡内的所有玩家
   	 local AllPlayer = UGCLevelFlowSystem.GetAllPlayerControllerInCurrentLevel()

	-- 每个玩家进行关底商店
	if AllPlayer and #AllPlayer > 0 then
		for k, v in pairs(AllPlayer) do
			v:CallShop()
		end
	else
		print("UGCLevelReward:LuaExecute AllPlayer is nil")
	end

	-- 关底奖励完成，进入结算
	self:OnFinish()
end

return UGCLevelReward
```

### 示例 2

```lua
-- UGCSettlement.lua

local UGCSettlement = {}

function UGCSettlement:LuaExecuteWithFinish(_, IsFinish)
	-- 获取当前关卡内的所有玩家
   	 local AllPlayer = UGCLevelFlowSystem.GetAllPlayerControllerInCurrentLevel()

	-- 每个玩家进行结算
	if AllPlayer and #AllPlayer > 0 then
		for k, v in pairs(AllPlayer) do
			v:CallSettle()
		end
	else
		print("UGCSettlement:LuaExecuteWithFinish AllPlayer is nil")
	end

	-- 关底结算完成，跳转到下个关卡
	self:OnFinish()
end

return UGCSettlement
```

### 示例 3

```lua
-- UGCResetData.lua

local UGCResetData = {}

function UGCResetData:LuaExecute()
	-- 获取当前关卡内的所有玩家
   	 local AllPlayer = UGCLevelFlowSystem.GetAllPlayerControllerInCurrentLevel()

	-- 每个玩家进行重置数据
	if AllPlayer and #AllPlayer > 0 then
		for k, v in pairs(AllPlayer) do
			v:CallReset()
		end
	else
		print("UGCSettlement:LuaExecuteWithFinish AllPlayer is nil")
	end
end

return UGCResetData
```

### 示例 4

```lua
-- UGCTimeOut.lua

local UGCTimeOut = {}

function UGCTimeOut:LuaExecute()
    -- 获取当前游戏内的所有玩家
    local AllPlayer = UGCLevelFlowSystem.GetAllPlayerControllerInCurrentLevel()

    if AllPlayer and #AllPlayer > 0 then
        for k, Player in pairs(AllPlayer) do
            -- 如果超时，逐个玩家调用失败结算，设置下面变量后会自动弹出超时失败结算界面
            if not Player.SettleParams.bIsSettled then
                Player.SettleParams.bIsSettled = true
                Player.SettleParams.bIsFinished = false
                DOREPONCE(Player, "SettleParams")
            end
        end
    else
        print("UGCTimeOut:LuaExecute AllPlayer is nil")
    end

end

return UGCTimeOut
```

### 示例 5

```lua
-- UGCSettlement.lua

local UGCSettlement = {}

function UGCSettlement:LuaExecuteWithFinish(_, IsFinish)

    -- 获取当前游戏内的所有玩家
    local AllPlayer = UGCLevelFlowSystem.GetAllPlayerControllerInCurrentLevel()
    if AllPlayer and #AllPlayer > 0 then
        for k, Player in pairs(AllPlayer) do

            -- 在玩家结算的时候更新游戏时间
            Player.PlayerState:UpdateGameTime()

            -- 通知玩家调出结算UI
            Player.SettleParams.bIsSettled = true
            Player.SettleParams.bIsFinished = true
            DOREPONCE(Player, "SettleParams")
        end
    else
        print("UGCSettlement:LuaExecuteWithFinish AllPlayer is nil")
    end

end

return UGCSettlement
```

### 示例 8

```lua
-- PortalDoor.lua

---@field Trigger UCapsuleComponent
local PortalDoor = { };

function PortalDoor:ReceiveBeginPlay()

    PortalDoor.SuperClass.ReceiveBeginPlay(self)
    if UGCGameSystem.IsServer() then
        self:InitDoor()
    end

end

function PortalDoor:InitDoor()
    self.Trigger.OnComponentBeginOverlap:Add(self.OnBeginOverlap, self)
end

function PortalDoor:ReceiveEndPlay()
    print(string.format("[PortalDoor:ReceiveEndPlay]"))
    self.Trigger.OnComponentBeginOverlap:Remove(self.OnBeginOverlap, self)
end

function PortalDoor:OnBeginOverlap(OverlappedComp, Actor, OtherComp, OtherBodyIndex, bFromSweep, SweepResult)

    -- 当玩家的Pawn和传送门折叠后，触发传送玩家
    UGCLevelFlowSystem.GoToNextLevelForOnePlayer(UGCGameSystem.GetPlayerControllerByPlayerKey(Actor.PlayerKey))

end

return PortalDoor
```

### 示例 1

```lua
---@class BP_UGCCustomSightLine_TopDown_C:UUGCCustomModeSightLine
local BP_UGCCustomSightLine_TopDown = {}

--更新枪械瞄准线特效
---@param ShootWeapon ASTExtraShootWeapon @枪械对象
---@param Range float @瞄准线长度
---@param Angle float @瞄准线角度
---@return UParticleSystemComponent @瞄准线特效组件
function BP_UGCCustomSightLine_TopDown:UpdateEffect(ShootWeapon, Range, Angle)
    local Effect = BP_UGCCustomSightLine_TopDown.SuperClass.UpdateEffect(self, ShootWeapon, Range, Angle)
    local Character = ShootWeapon:GetOwnerPlayerController():GetControlledPawn()
    local bSwitchWeapon = UGCPlayerPawnSystem.HasPawnState(Character, EPawnState.SwitchWeapon)
    Effect:SetActive(!bSwitchWeapon, false)
    return Effect
end

--根据枪械对象获取瞄准线特效资产模板
---@param ShootWeapon ASTExtraShootWeapon @枪械对象
---@return UParticleSystem @瞄准线特效模板
function BP_UGCCustomSightLine_TopDown:FindSightLineTemplateByShootWeapon(ShootWeapon)
    return BP_UGCCustomSightLine_TopDown.SuperClass.FindSightLineTemplateByShootWeapon(self, ShootWeapon)
end

return BP_UGCCustomSightLine_TopDown
```

```lua
--更新枪械瞄准线特效

function BP_SD:UpdateEffect(ShootWeapon, Range, Angle)
    local Effect = BP_SD.SuperClass.UpdateEffect(self, ShootWeapon, Range, Angle)
    local Controller = ShootWeapon:GetOwnerPlayerController()
    local Character = UGCPlayerControllerSystem.GetPlayerCharacter(Controller)
    Effect:K2_AttachToComponent(Character.RootComponent, "NAME_None", EAttachmentRule.KeepRelative, EAttachmentRule.KeepRelative, EAttachmentRule.KeepWorld, true)

    -- local bSwitchWeapon = UGCPlayerPawnSystem.HasPawnState(Character, EPawnState.SwitchWeapon)

    return Effect
end
```

```lua
-- 转向，执行时候会朝向目标队列中的第一个目标
function TopDownAutoAim:Turn_Lua()
    local Targets = self:GetSelectTargetActor(EPESkillSelectTarget.E_PESKILL_PickerType_AllTarget)
    local NetOwnerActor = self:GetNetOwnerActor()
    if Targets ~= nil and #Targets > 0 then
        local Target = Targets[1]
        if Target ~= nil then
            local TargetLocation = Target:K2_GetActorLocation()
            local NetOwnerLocation = NetOwnerActor:K2_GetActorLocation()
            local Direction = KismetMathLibrary.Subtract_VectorVector(TargetLocation, NetOwnerLocation)
            local _Rotator = STExtraBlueprintFunctionLibrary.Vector2Rotator(Direction)
            local NewRotator = Rotator.New(0, 0, _Rotator.Yaw)
            -- ugcprint_format("NewRotator: %s", _Rotator.Yaw)
            STExtraBlueprintFunctionLibrary.SetControlRotationByReason(NetOwnerActor,NewRotator,EUGCCustomModeInputReason.UCMIR_AutoAim)
            return
        end
    end
    STExtraBlueprintFunctionLibrary.ResetControlRotationReason(NetOwnerActor, EUGCCustomModeInputReason.UCMIR_AutoAim)
end
```

```lua
-- 检查当前阶段
if self.GameState.CurrentGameStatus == EGameStatus.Prepare then
    -- 允许玩家选择队伍和装备
end

-- 切换到战斗阶段
self.GameState:SetStatus(EGameStatus.Battle, 300)

-- 判断是否为结算阶段
if self.GameState.CurrentGameStatus == EGameStatus.Result then
    -- 显示结算界面
end
```

### 示例 3

```lua
-- 在 UI 脚本中订阅事件
function MyWidget:OnWidgetShow()
    UGCEventSystem:RegisterEvent(
        CTCompetitioClientEvent.GameStatusChanged,
        self,
        self.OnGameStatusChanged
    )
end

function MyWidget:OnGameStatusChanged(NewStatus)
    if NewStatus == EGameStatus.Prepare then
        -- 显示准备阶段 UI
        self:ShowPrepareUI()
    elseif NewStatus == EGameStatus.Battle then
        -- 显示战斗阶段 UI
        self:ShowBattleUI()
    elseif NewStatus == EGameStatus.Result then
        -- 显示结算界面
        self:ShowResultUI()
    end
end
```

```lua
    self.CountdownText:SetText(string.format("%02d:%02d",

        math.floor(RemainingTime % 60)))
end
```

```lua
function MyWidget:OnPlayerScoreChanged(PlayerScore)
    -- 清空现有数据
    self:ClearScoreBoard()
   
    -- 遍历所有玩家战绩
    for PlayerKey, ScoreData in pairs(PlayerScore) do
        local PS = UGCGameSystem.GetPlayerStateByPlayerKey(PlayerKey)
        if PS then
            self:AddScoreEntry({
                Name = PS.PlayerName,
                KillCount = ScoreData.KillCount,
                DeathCount = ScoreData.DeathCount,
                TeamID = ScoreData.TeamID
            })
        end
    end
end
```

### 示例 8

```lua
function MyWidget:OnTeamScoreChanged(TeamScore)
    -- 更新红队积分显示
    self.RedTeamScoreText:SetText(tostring(TeamScore[ETeamType.Red]))
   
    -- 更新蓝队积分显示
    self.BlueTeamScoreText:SetText(tostring(TeamScore[ETeamType.Blue]))
   
    -- 检查是否达到胜利分数
    if TeamScore[ETeamType.Red] >= self.GameState.WinScore then
        self:ShowTeamScoreWarning(ETeamType.Red)
    elseif TeamScore[ETeamType.Blue] >= self.GameState.WinScore then
        self:ShowTeamScoreWarning(ETeamType.Blue)
    end
end
```

### 示例 9

```lua
function MyWidget:OnGameEndEvent(BattleResult)
    -- 判定获胜队伍
    if BattleResult.WinTeam == ETeamType.Red then
        self.WinnerText:SetText("红队获胜！")
        self.WinnerIcon:SetColor(FLinearColor(1.0, 0.2, 0.2, 1.0))  -- 红色
    elseif BattleResult.WinTeam == ETeamType.Blue then
        self.WinnerText:SetText("蓝队获胜！")
        self.WinnerIcon:SetColor(FLinearColor(0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 1.0))  -- 蓝色
    else
        self.WinnerText:SetText("平局！")
        self.WinnerIcon:SetColor(FLinearColor(0.8, 0.8, 0.8, 1.0))  -- 灰色
    end
   
    -- 显示最终战绩
    self:ShowFinalScoreboard(BattleResult.PlayerScore)
   
    -- 判定 MVP（击杀数最多的玩家）
    local MVPPlayerKey = nil
    local MaxKillCount = 0
   
    for PlayerKey, ScoreData in pairs(BattleResult.PlayerScore) do
        if ScoreData.KillCount > MaxKillCount then
            MaxKillCount = ScoreData.KillCount
            MVPPlayerKey = PlayerKey
        end
    end
   
    if MVPPlayerKey then
        local PS = UGCGameSystem.GetPlayerStateByPlayerKey(MVPPlayerKey)
        if PS then
            self:MVPPlayerText:SetText("MVP: " .. PS.PlayerName)
        end
    end
end
```

```lua
params:

function UGCGameState:UpdateScore(Killer, VictimPlayer)
    -- 原有战绩更新逻辑...
   
    -- 发布击杀通知事件
    if Killer and VictimPlayer then
        local KillerPS = UGCGameSystem.GetPlayerStateByPlayerKey(Killer.PlayerKey)
        local VictimPS = UGCGameSystem.GetPlayerStateByPlayerKey(VictimPlayer.PlayerKey)
       
        if KillerPS and VictimPS then
            local KillData = {

                VictimName = VictimPS.PlayerName,
                WeaponName = "M416",  -- 可根据实际情况获取
                IsHeadshot = false
            }
           
            UGCEventSystem:SendEvent(CTCompetitioClientEvent.KillNotification, KillData)
        end
    end
end

        CTCompetitioClientEvent.KillNotification,
        self,
        self.OnKillNotification
    )
end

function MyWidget:OnKillNotification(KillData)
    local Message = string.format("%s 击杀了 %s", KillData.KillerName, KillData.VictimName)
    if KillData.IsHeadshot then
        Message = Message .. " [爆头！]"
    end
   
    self:AddKillMessage(Message)
end
```

```lua
-- 1. 在 CTCompetitionEnum.lua 中添加
ETeamType =
{
    Initial = 0,
    Red = 1,
    Blue = 2,
    Green = 3  -- 绿队
}

-- 2. 在 UGCGameState.lua 中初始化
UGCGameState.TeamCurPlayerKeys[ETeamType.Red] = {}
UGCGameState.TeamCurPlayerKeys[ETeamType.Blue] = {}
UGCGameState.TeamCurPlayerKeys[ETeamType.Green] = {}  -- 新增
UGCGameState.TeamScore[ETeamType.Red] = 0
UGCGameState.TeamScore[ETeamType.Blue] = 0
UGCGameState.TeamScore[ETeamType.Green] = 0  -- 新增

-- 3. 修改 UpdateTeamData 函数
function UGCGameState:UpdateTeamData()
    -- 红队
    self.TeamCurPlayerKeys[ETeamType.Red] = {}
    self.TeamCurPlayerUIDs[ETeamType.Red] = {}
    for k,v in ipairs(UGCTeamSystem.GetPlayerKeysByTeamID(ETeamType.Red) or {}) do
        table.insert(self.TeamCurPlayerKeys[ETeamType.Red], v)
        local PS = UGCGameSystem.GetPlayerStateByPlayerKey(v)
        if PS then
            table.insert(self.TeamCurPlayerUIDs[ETeamType.Red], PS.UID)
        end
    end
   
    -- 蓝队
    self.TeamCurPlayerKeys[ETeamType.Blue] = {}
    self.TeamCurPlayerUIDs[ETeamType.Blue] = {}
    for k,v in ipairs(UGCTeamSystem.GetPlayerKeysByTeamID(ETeamType.Blue) or {}) do
        table.insert(self.TeamCurPlayerKeys[ETeamType.Blue], v)
        local PS = UGCGameSystem.GetPlayerStateByPlayerKey(v)
        if PS then
            table.insert(self.TeamCurPlayerUIDs[ETeamType.Blue], PS.UID)
        end
    end
   
    -- 绿队（新增）
    self.TeamCurPlayerKeys[ETeamType.Green] = {}
    self.TeamCurPlayerUIDs[ETeamType.Green] = {}
    for k,v in ipairs(UGCTeamSystem.GetPlayerKeysByTeamID(ETeamType.Green) or {}) do
        table.insert(self.TeamCurPlayerKeys[ETeamType.Green], v)
        local PS = UGCGameSystem.GetPlayerStateByPlayerKey(v)
        if PS then
            table.insert(self.TeamCurPlayerUIDs[ETeamType.Green], PS.UID)
        end
    end
   
    -- 同步数据
    UnrealNetwork.RepLazyProperty(self, "TeamCurPlayerKeys")
    UnrealNetwork.RepLazyProperty(self, "TeamCurPlayerUIDs")
end
```

## 游戏模式核心逻辑详解

> 来源: 玩法案例及模板/团竞模板/团竞2.0玩法 (id:20358)

### 示例 1

```lua
UGCGameMode.GeneralRangeSettings = {
    MaxPlayerNumPerTeam = 8,			-- 每队最大人数
    MinPlayerNumPerTeam = 1,			-- 每队最小人数
		MaxHealth = 30000,						-- 最大血量值
		MinhEalth = 50,								-- 最小血量值
		MaxSpeedScale = 3,						-- 最大速度缩放值
		MinSpeedScale = 0.1,					-- 最小速度缩放值
		MaxGravityScale = 2,					-- 最大重力缩放值
		MinGravityScale = 0.5 				-- 最小重力缩放值
    MaxReadyTime = 60,						-- 最大准备时间
    MinReadyTime = 10,						-- 最小准备时间
    MaxFightTime = 600,						-- 最大战斗时间
    MinFightTime = 90,						-- 最小战斗时间
    MaxTargetScore = 200,					-- 最大目标分数
    MinTargetScore = 10,					-- 最小目标分数
    MaxRespawnWaitingTime = 10,		-- 最大复活等待
    MinRespawnWaitingTime = 0.5,	-- 最小复活等待
    MaxRespawnInvincibleTime = 5,	-- 最大无敌时间
    MinRespawnInvincibleTime = 0,	-- 最小无敌时间
    MinKillScore = 0							-- 最小击杀得分
}
```

```lua
UGCGameMode.GeneralGameSettings = {
    ClampPrepareTime = 15,      -- 实际准备时间
    ClampPlayerNum = 4,         -- 实际每队人数
    ClampWinScore = 50,         -- 实际胜利分数
    ClampKillScore = 0,         -- 实际击杀得分
    ClampFightTime = 300,       -- 实际战斗时间
    ClampRespawnWaitingTime = 3, -- 实际复活等待
    ClampRespawnInvincibleTime = 3, -- 实际无敌时间

    ClampIsEnablePlayerWeaponConfig = true -- 实际是否允许配装
```

### 示例 3

```lua
function UGCGameMode:ReceiveBeginPlay()
    ugcprint("UGCGameMode:ReceiveBeginPlay")
   
    -- 初始化所有 Clamp 值
    self:InitAllClampNumAtGeneralRangeSettings()
   
    -- 订阅死亡事件
    UGCGenericMessageSystem.ListenGlobalMessage(
        self,
        UGCGenericMessageSystem.Messages.UGC.PlayerPawn.PawnDefeat,
        self,
        self.OnPawnDefeat
    )
end
```

### 示例 4

```lua
function UGCGameMode:InitAllClampNumAtGeneralRangeSettings()
    -- 准备时间 Clamp
    self.GeneralGameSettings.ClampPrepareTime = math.max(
        self.GeneralRangeSettings.MinReadyTime,
        math.min(self.GeneralRangeSettings.MaxReadyTime, self.CFG_PrepareTime)
    )
   
    -- 每队人数 Clamp
    self.GeneralGameSettings.ClampPlayerNum = math.max(
        self.GeneralRangeSettings.MinPlayerNumPerTeam,
        math.min(self.GeneralRangeSettings.MaxPlayerNumPerTeam, self.CFG_MaxTeamMember)
    )
    self.GameState.TeamMaxPlayerNum = self.GeneralGameSettings.ClampPlayerNum
    UnrealNetwork.RepLazyProperty(self.GameState, "TeamMaxPlayerNum")
   
    -- 胜利分数 Clamp
    self.GeneralGameSettings.ClampWinScore = math.max(
        self.GeneralRangeSettings.MinTargetScore,
        math.min(self.GeneralRangeSettings.MaxTargetScore, self.CFG_WinScore)
    )
    self.GameState.WinScore = self.GeneralGameSettings.ClampWinScore
    UnrealNetwork.RepLazyProperty(self.GameState, "WinScore")
   
    -- ... 其他参数初始化
end
```

```lua
    -- 检查是否到达状态切换时间
    if WorldTime >= self.GameState.NextStatusTimeStamp then
        self:GotoNextStatus()
    end
end
```

```lua
function UGCGameMode:UGC_PlayerLoginEvent(PlayerController)
    ugcprint("UGCGameMode:UGC_PlayerLoginEvent PlayerKey="..PlayerController.PlayerKey)
   
    -- 检查玩家是否已存在
    for _, key in ipairs(self.PlayerKeyList) do
        if key == PlayerController.PlayerKey then
            return
        end
    end
   
    -- 设置初始队伍
    UGCTeamSystem.ChangePlayerTeamID(PlayerController.PlayerKey, ETeamType.Initial)
   
    -- 加入玩家列表
    table.insert(self.PlayerKeyList, PlayerController.PlayerKey)
   
    -- 第一个玩家加入后开始游戏
    if self.GameState.CurrentGameStatus == EGameStatus.Initial then
        self.GameState:SetStatus(EGameStatus.Prepare, self.GeneralGameSettings.ClampPrepareTime)
    end
end
```

```lua
    table.remove(self.PlayerKeyList, PlayerController.PlayerKey)
end
```

### 示例 9

```lua
function UGCGameMode:GotoNextStatus()
    if self.GameState.CurrentGameStatus == EGameStatus.Prepare then
        -- 为未选队伍的玩家随机分配队伍
        self:RandomTeamForPlayerWithoutTeam()
       
        -- 检查游戏是否结束
        if self:CheckGameEnd() then
            self.GameState:SetStatus(EGameStatus.Result, self.GeneralGameSettings.ClampFightTime)
        else
            -- 进入战斗阶段
            self.GameState:SetStatus(EGameStatus.Battle, self.GeneralGameSettings.ClampFightTime)
           
            -- 重建所有玩家
            for i,v in ipairs(self.GameState.TeamCurPlayerKeys) do
                for k,PlayerKey in pairs(v) do
                    local PC = UGCGameSystem.GetPlayerControllerByPlayerKey(PlayerKey)
                    if PC and PC.Pawn then
                        PC.Pawn:SetLifeSpan(0.001)
                    end
                    UGCGameSystem.RespawnPlayer(PlayerKey)
                end
            end
           
            -- 初始化全部玩家分数
            self.GameState:InitAllPlayerScore()
        end
       
    elseif self.GameState.CurrentGameStatus == EGameStatus.Battle then
        -- 进入结算阶段
        self.GameState:SetStatus(EGameStatus.Result, self.CFG_ResultTime)
    end
end
```

### 示例 10

```lua
function UGCGameMode:PlayerChangeTeam(PlayerKey, NewTeamID)
    ugcprint("UGCGameMode:PlayerChangeTeam "..tostring(PlayerKey).." NewTeamID = "..tostring(NewTeamID))
   
    -- 验证队伍 ID
    if NewTeamID ~= ETeamType.Red and NewTeamID ~= ETeamType.Blue then
        ugcprint("UGCGameMode:PlayerChangeTeam Team with Invalid TeamID")
        return
    end
   
    -- 检查队伍是否已满员
    local TeamMemberNum = UGCTeamSystem.GetTeamSizeByID(NewTeamID)
    if TeamMemberNum >= self.GeneralGameSettings.ClampPlayerNum then
        ugcprint("UGCGameMode:PlayerChangeTeam Team without pos")
        return
    end
   
    local PC = UGCGameSystem.GetPlayerControllerByPlayerKey(PlayerKey)
   
    -- 避免重复切换
    if PC and PC.TeamID == NewTeamID then
        ugcprint("UGCGameMode:PlayerChangeTeam Team with Same TeamID")
        self.GameState:UpdateTeamData()
        return
    end
   
    -- 切换队伍
    UGCTeamSystem.ChangePlayerTeamID(PlayerKey, NewTeamID)
    self.GameState:UpdateTeamData()
   
    -- 复活到新出生点
    if PC and PC.Pawn then
        PC.Pawn:SetLifeSpan(0.001)
    end
    UGCGameSystem.RespawnPlayer(PlayerKey)
end
```

### 示例 11

```lua
function UGCGameMode:RandomTeamForPlayerWithoutTeam()
    for i=1, #self.PlayerKeyList do
        local PlayerKey = self.PlayerKeyList[i]
        local PC = UGCGameSystem.GetPlayerControllerByPlayerKey(PlayerKey)
       
        if PC and PC.TeamID == ETeamType.Initial then
            -- 优先分配玩家少的队伍
            if #self.GameState.TeamCurPlayerKeys[ETeamType.Red] <
               #self.GameState.TeamCurPlayerKeys[ETeamType.Blue] then
                self:PlayerChangeTeam(PlayerKey, ETeamType.Red)
            else
                self:PlayerChangeTeam(PlayerKey, ETeamType.Blue)
            end
        end
    end
end
```

### 示例 12

```lua
function UGCGameMode:OnPawnDefeat(VictimPlayerKey, InstigatorPlayerKey, DamageType)
    -- 结算阶段不计分
    if EGameStatus.Result == self.GameState.CurrentGameStatus then
        return
    end
   
    local Killer = UGCGameSystem.GetPlayerControllerByPlayerKey(InstigatorPlayerKey)
    local VictimPlayer = UGCGameSystem.GetPlayerControllerByPlayerKey(VictimPlayerKey)
   
    -- 更新积分
    self.GameState:UpdateScore(Killer, VictimPlayer)
   
    -- 复活
    if VictimPlayer then
        UGCTimerUtility.CreateLuaTimer(
            self.GeneralGameSettings.ClampRespawnWaitingTime,
            function()
                UGCGameSystem.RespawnPlayer(VictimPlayer.PlayerKey)
            end,
            false
        )
    end
   
    -- 检测结算条件
    if self:CheckGameEnd() then
        self.GameState:SetStatus(EGameStatus.Result, self.CFG_ResultTime)
    end
end
```

### 示例 13

```lua
function UGCGameMode:CheckGameEnd()
    -- 检查队伍是否有人
    if #self.GameState.TeamCurPlayerKeys[ETeamType.Red] == 0 or
       #self.GameState.TeamCurPlayerKeys[ETeamType.Blue] == 0 then
        return true
    end
   
    -- 检查是否达到胜利分数
    if self.GameState.TeamScore[ETeamType.Red] >= self.GameState.WinScore or
       self.GameState.TeamScore[ETeamType.Blue] >= self.GameState.WinScore then
        return true
    end
   
    return false
end
```

### 示例 14

```lua
function UGCGameMode:UGC_PlayerRespawnEvent(RespawnedController)
    ugcprint("UGCGameMode:UGC_PlayerRespawnEvent PlayerKey="..RespawnedController.PlayerKey)
   
    if self.GameState.CurrentGameStatus == EGameStatus.Battle then
        -- 组织武器列表
        RespawnedController:OrganizeWeaponList()
        local WeaponList = RespawnedController.PlayerState.WeaponList
        log_tree("UGCGameMode:UGC_PlayerRespawnEvent:",WeaponList)
       
        -- 发放初始装备
        self:AddInitialEquipment(RespawnedController, WeaponList)
       
        -- 挂无敌 Buff
        local BuffPath = UGCGameSystem.GetUGCResourcesFullPath("Asset/Blueprint/Buffs/Buff_invincible.Buff_invincible_C")
        local BuffClass = UE.LoadClass(BuffPath)
        UGCPersistEffectSystem.AddBuffByClass(RespawnedController.Pawn, BuffClass)
       
        -- 设置无敌时间
        local InvincibleBuffList = UGCPersistEffectSystem.GetBuffsByClass(RespawnedController.Pawn, BuffClass)
        for _, v in pairs(InvincibleBuffList) do
            v:SetApplyTime(math.max(
                self.GeneralRangeSettings.MinRespawnInvincibleTime,
                math.min(self.GeneralRangeSettings.MaxRespawnInvincibleTime, v:GetApplyTime())
            ))
        end
       
        -- 重置伤害来源列表
        RespawnedController.PlayerState.DamagePlayerKeys = {}
    end
end
```

### 示例 15

```lua
function UGCGameMode:AddInitialEquipment(RespawnedController, ItemList)
    for _, v in pairs(ItemList) do
        local ItemData = UGCItemSystem.GetItemData(v.ItemID)
       
        -- 添加主武器
        self:AddInitalItem(RespawnedController.Pawn, v.ItemID, v.Count)
       
        -- 添加配件
        for _, value in pairs(v.SubItemList) do
            self:AddInitalItem(RespawnedController.Pawn, value.ItemID, value.Count)
        end
    end
end
```

### 示例 16

```lua
function UGCGameMode:AddInitalItem(Pawn, ItemID, TotalCount)
    local ItemConfig = UGCItemSystem.GetItemData(ItemID)
   
    -- 检查物品有效性
    if ItemConfig.ItemID == 0 then
        print(string.format("UGCGameMode:AddInitalItem ItemID=%d not found", ItemID))
        return
    end
   
    -- 数量不超限直接添加
    if TotalCount <= ItemConfig.MaxCount then
        UGCBackPackSystem.AddItem(Pawn, ItemID, TotalCount)
        return
    end
   
    -- 数量超限拆分堆叠
    local StackCount = math.floor(TotalCount / ItemConfig.MaxCount)
    local LastStackCount = TotalCount % ItemConfig.MaxCount
   
    for i = 1, StackCount do
        UGCBackPackSystem.AddItem(Pawn, ItemID, ItemConfig.MaxCount)
    end
   
    if LastStackCount > 0 then
        UGCBackPackSystem.AddItem(Pawn, ItemID, LastStackCount)
    end
end
```

```lua
function UGCGameMode:FindPlayerStart(Player, IncomingName)
    local TeamID = Player.TeamID
    ugcprint("UGCGameMode:FindPlayerStart TeamID="..TeamID)
   
    if TeamID < 0 then
        TeamID = 1
    end
   
    local PlayerStartClass = UGCObjectUtility.LoadClass(
        "/Game/BluePrints/Player/PlayerStart/BP_STPlayerStart.BP_STPlayerStart_C"
    )
    local PlayerStartList = UGCObjectUtility.GetAllActorsOfClass(self, PlayerStartClass)
   
    for i=1, #PlayerStartList do
        if PlayerStartList[i] and PlayerStartList[i].PlayerBornPointID == TeamID then
            return PlayerStartList[i]
        end
    end
end
```

## 游戏状态与数据同步详解

> 来源: 玩法案例及模板/团竞模板/团竞2.0玩法 (id:20359)

### 示例 1

```lua
local UGCGameState = {
    -- 当前游戏阶段
    CurrentGameStatus = EGameStatus.Initial,
    -- 当前阶段结束时间
    NextStatusTimeStamp = 0,
   
    -- 队伍数据
    TeamCurPlayerKeys = {},      -- 当前队伍玩家 Key 列表
    TeamCurPlayerUIDs = {},      -- 当前队伍玩家 UID 列表
    TeamMaxPlayerNum = 4,        -- 每队最大人数

    -- 战绩数据
    WinScore = 20,               -- 胜利分数
    TeamScore = {},             -- 队伍积分
    PlayerScore = {},           -- 玩家战绩
   
    -- 结算数据
    BattleResult = {},          -- 最终战绩
   
    -- 击杀积分
    KillScore = 1,
   
    -- 装备配置
    EquipSelectRange = {},       -- 装备选择范围
    IsEquipSelectDataSet = false,

}
```

```lua
        {"TeamCurPlayerKeys", "Lazy"},          -- 队伍玩家列表
        {"TeamCurPlayerUIDs", "Lazy"},          -- 队伍 UID 列表
        {"TeamMaxPlayerNum", "Lazy"},           -- 每队最大人数
        {"PlayerScore", "Lazy"},               -- 玩家战绩
        {"WinScore", "Lazy"},                  -- 胜利分数
        {"BattleResult", "Lazy"},              -- 结算数据
        {"KillScore", "Lazy"},                 -- 击杀得分

    }
end
```

### 示例 4

```lua
function UGCGameState:SetStatus(InStatus, InDuration)
    self.CurrentGameStatus = InStatus
    self.NextStatusTimeStamp = self:GetWorldTime() + InDuration
   
    ugcprint("UGCGameMode:SetStatus InStatus="..tostring(InStatus).." InDuration="..tostring(InDuration).." NextStatusTimeStamp="..tostring(self.NextStatusTimeStamp))
   
    -- 根据阶段设置无敌状态
    if self.CurrentGameStatus == EGameStatus.Prepare then
        local AllPawn = UGCGameSystem.GetAllPlayerPawn()
        for _, Pawn in ipairs(AllPawn) do
            UGCPlayerPawnSystem.SetIsInvincible(Pawn, true)
        end
    else
        local AllPawn = UGCGameSystem.GetAllPlayerPawn()
        for _, Pawn in ipairs(AllPawn) do
            UGCPlayerPawnSystem.SetIsInvincible(Pawn, false)
        end
    end
   
    -- 结算阶段准备
    if self.CurrentGameStatus == EGameStatus.Result then
        -- 判定胜利队伍
        if self.TeamScore[ETeamType.Red] == self.TeamScore[ETeamType.Blue] then
            self.BattleResult.WinTeam = 0
        elseif self.TeamScore[ETeamType.Red] > self.TeamScore[ETeamType.Blue] then
            self.BattleResult.WinTeam = ETeamType.Red
        else
            self.BattleResult.WinTeam = ETeamType.Blue
        end
       
        -- 下发战绩数据
        self.BattleResult.PlayerScore = self.PlayerScore
        log_tree("UGCGameMode:Send BattleResult", self.BattleResult)
        UnrealNetwork.RepLazyProperty(self, "BattleResult")
    end
   
    -- 同步数据到客户端
    UnrealNetwork.RepLazyProperty(self, "CurrentGameStatus")
    UnrealNetwork.RepLazyProperty(self, "NextStatusTimeStamp")
end
```

### 示例 6

```lua
function UGCGameState:OnGameStateChange(LastGameStatus)
    ugcprint("UGCGameState:OnGameStateChange LastGameStatus="..tostring(LastGameStatus).." CurrentGameStatus="..tostring(self.CurrentGameStatus))
   
    if self.CurrentGameStatus == EGameStatus.Prepare then
        -- 加载主界面
        local TeamMainUIPath = UGCMapInfoLib.GetRootLongPackagePath().. "Asset/UMG/TMode/UIBP/TMode_Main_UIBP.TMode_Main_UIBP_C"
        UGCWidgetManagerSystem.CreateWidgetAsync(TeamMainUIPath,
            function(Widget)
                if self.TeamMainUI == nil then
                    self.TeamMainUI = Widget
                    if UE.IsValid(self.TeamMainUI) then
                        self.TeamMainUI:AddToViewport(10060)
                        -- 允许选队时立即显示队伍选择 UI
                        if self.IsEnablePlayerTeamSelect then
                            self.TeamMainUI:OnClickSelectTeam()
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        )
       
    elseif self.CurrentGameStatus == EGameStatus.Battle then
        -- 从准备阶段切换时，加载加载界面
        if LastGameStatus == EGameStatus.Prepare then
            local TeamLoadingUIPath = UGCMapInfoLib.GetRootLongPackagePath().. "Asset/UMG/TMode/UIBP/TMode_Loading_UIBP.TMode_Loading_UIBP_C"
            UGCWidgetManagerSystem.CreateWidgetAsync(TeamLoadingUIPath,
                function(Widget)
                    self.LoadingUI = Widget
                    if UE.IsValid(self.LoadingUI) then
                        self.LoadingUI:AddToViewport(11000)
                    end
                end
            )
        end
       
        -- 加载记分板（仅加载一次）
        if not self.ScoreBoardUI then
            local ScoreBoardUIPath = UGCMapInfoLib.GetRootLongPackagePath().. "Asset/UMG/TMode/UIBP/TMode_ScoreBoard_UIBP.TMode_ScoreBoard_UIBP_C"
            UGCWidgetManagerSystem.CreateWidgetAsync(ScoreBoardUIPath,
                function(Widget)
                    self.ScoreBoardUI = Widget
                    -- 挂到主 UI 的 UISlot 上
                    local AnchorData = UGCObjectUtility.NewStruct("AnchorData")
                    local Anchors = UGCObjectUtility.NewStruct("Anchors");
                    local Minimum = UGCObjectUtility.NewStruct("Vector2D")
                    local Maximum = UGCObjectUtility.NewStruct("Vector2D")
                    Minimum.X, Minimum.Y = 0, 0
                    Maximum.X, Maximum.Y = 1, 1
                    Anchors.Minimum = Minimum
                    Anchors.Maximum = Maximum
                    AnchorData.Anchors = Anchors;
                    UGCWidgetManagerSystem.AddChildToUISlotByWidget(self.ScoreBoardUI, 'UI.UISlot.MainUISlot_Low', 0, AnchorData)
                end
            )
        end
       
    elseif self.CurrentGameStatus == EGameStatus.Result then
        -- 加载结算界面
        if not self.ResultUI then    
            local ResultUIPath = UGCMapInfoLib.GetRootLongPackagePath().. "Asset/UMG/TMode/UIBP/TMode_Result_Both_UIBP.TMode_Result_Both_UIBP_C"
            UGCWidgetManagerSystem.CreateWidgetAsync(ResultUIPath,
                function(Widget)                      
                    self.ResultUI = Widget
                    if UE.IsValid(self.ResultUI) then
                        UGCWidgetManagerSystem.GetMainUI():SetVisibility(ESlateVisibility.Collapsed)
                        self.ResultUI:AddToViewport(11040)
                    end
                end
            )
        end
    end
   
    -- 加载通用提示 UI（仅加载一次）
    if not self.CommonTips then
        local CommonTipsUIPath = UGCMapInfoLib.GetRootLongPackagePath().. "Asset/UMG/TMode/UIBP/TMode_CommonTips_UIBP.TMode_CommonTips_UIBP_C"
        UGCWidgetManagerSystem.CreateWidgetAsync(CommonTipsUIPath,
            function(Widget)
                self.CommonTips = Widget
                if UE.IsValid(self.CommonTips) then
                    self.CommonTips:AddToViewport(11070)
                end
            end
        )
    end
end
```

### 示例 7

```lua
function UGCGameState:UpdateTeamData()
    -- 更新红队数据
    self.TeamCurPlayerKeys[ETeamType.Red] = {}
    self.TeamCurPlayerUIDs[ETeamType.Red] = {}
    for k,v in ipairs(UGCTeamSystem.GetPlayerKeysByTeamID(ETeamType.Red) or {}) do
        table.insert(self.TeamCurPlayerKeys[ETeamType.Red], v)
       
        local PS = UGCGameSystem.GetPlayerStateByPlayerKey(v)
        if PS then
            table.insert(self.TeamCurPlayerUIDs[ETeamType.Red], PS.UID)
        end
    end
   
    -- 更新蓝队数据
    self.TeamCurPlayerKeys[ETeamType.Blue] = {}
    self.TeamCurPlayerUIDs[ETeamType.Blue] = {}
    for k,v in ipairs(UGCTeamSystem.GetPlayerKeysByTeamID(ETeamType.Blue) or {}) do
        table.insert(self.TeamCurPlayerKeys[ETeamType.Blue], v)
       
        local PS = UGCGameSystem.GetPlayerStateByPlayerKey(v)
        if PS then
            table.insert(self.TeamCurPlayerUIDs[ETeamType.Blue], PS.UID)
        end
    end
   
    log_tree("UpdateTeamData TeamCurPlayerKeys", self.TeamCurPlayerKeys)
    log_tree("UpdateTeamData TeamCurPlayerUIDs", self.TeamCurPlayerUIDs)
   
    -- 同步到客户端
    UnrealNetwork.RepLazyProperty(self, "TeamCurPlayerKeys")
    UnrealNetwork.RepLazyProperty(self, "TeamCurPlayerUIDs")
end
```

### 示例 10

```lua
function UGCGameState:UpdateScore(Killer, VictimPlayer)
    local PlayerScore = self.PlayerScore
   
    -- 击杀者加分（必须不同队）
    if Killer and VictimPlayer and Killer.TeamID ~= VictimPlayer.TeamID then
        self:TryInitPlayerScore(Killer)
        PlayerScore[Killer.PlayerKey].KillCount = PlayerScore[Killer.PlayerKey].KillCount + 1
    end
   
    -- 被击杀者记录死亡
    if VictimPlayer then
        self:TryInitPlayerScore(VictimPlayer)
        PlayerScore[VictimPlayer.PlayerKey].DeathCount = PlayerScore[VictimPlayer.PlayerKey].DeathCount + 1
       
        -- 助攻判定（造成伤害的其他玩家）
        if VictimPlayer.PlayerState and next(VictimPlayer.PlayerState.DamagePlayerKeys) ~= nil then
            for k,v in pairs(VictimPlayer.PlayerState.DamagePlayerKeys) do
                if k ~= Killer.PlayerKey and k ~= VictimPlayer.PlayerKey then
                    local AssistPC = UGCGameSystem.GetPlayerControllerByPlayerKey(k)
                    if AssistPC then
                        self:TryInitPlayerScore(AssistPC)
                        PlayerScore[k].AssistCount = PlayerScore[k].AssistCount + 1
                    end
                    break
                end
            end
        end
    end
   
    log_tree("UGCGameState:UpdateScore", PlayerScore)
   
    -- 同步数据
    UnrealNetwork.RepLazyProperty(self, "PlayerScore")
   
    -- 更新队伍积分
    self:UpdateTeamScore()
end
```

```lua
function UGCGameState:OnRep_PlayerScore()
    log_tree("UGCGameState:OnRep_PlayerScore", self.PlayerScore)
   
    -- 发送玩家战绩变更事件
    UGCEventSystem:SendEvent(CTCompetitioClientEvent.PlayerScoreChanged, self.PlayerScore)
   
    -- 更新队伍积分
    self:UpdateTeamScore()

    UGCEventSystem:SendEvent(CTCompetitioClientEvent.TeamScoreChanged, self.TeamScore)
end
```

### 示例 12

```lua
function UGCGameState:UpdateTeamScore()
    self.TeamScore[ETeamType.Red] = 0
    self.TeamScore[ETeamType.Blue] = 0
   
    for k,v in pairs(self.PlayerScore) do
        if v.TeamID == ETeamType.Blue or v.TeamID == ETeamType.Red then
            self.TeamScore[v.TeamID] = self.TeamScore[v.TeamID] + v.KillCount * self.KillScore
        end
    end
   
    log_tree("UGCGameState:UpdateTeamScore", self.TeamScore)
end
```

```lua
function UGCGameState:InitAllPlayerScore()
    ugcprint("UGCGameState:InitAllPlayerScore")
    log_tree("UGCGameState:InitAllPlayerScore,TeamCurPlayerKeys", self.TeamCurPlayerKeys)
   
    for i,v in ipairs(self.TeamCurPlayerKeys) do
        for k,PlayerKey in pairs(v) do
            local PC = UGCGameSystem.GetPlayerControllerByPlayerKey(PlayerKey)
            -- 初始化单个玩家分数
            self:TryInitPlayerScore(PC)
        end
    end
   
    log_tree("UGCGameState:InitAllPlayerScore,PlayerScore", self.PlayerScore)
    UnrealNetwork.RepLazyProperty(self, "PlayerScore")
end
```

### 示例 14

```lua
function UGCGameState:TryInitPlayerScore(PC)
    local PlayerScore = self.PlayerScore
   
    if PlayerScore[PC.PlayerKey] == nil then
        PlayerScore[PC.PlayerKey] = {}
        PlayerScore[PC.PlayerKey].KillCount = 0
        PlayerScore[PC.PlayerKey].DeathCount = 0
        PlayerScore[PC.PlayerKey].AssistCount = 0
        PlayerScore[PC.PlayerKey].TeamID = PC.TeamID
        PlayerScore[PC.PlayerKey].UID = PC.PlayerState.UID
    end
end
```

### 示例 15

```lua
function UGCGameState:OnRep_BattleResult()
    if next(self.BattleResult) == nil then
        return
    end
   
    -- 重新计算队伍积分（确保数据一致性）
    local TeamScore = {}
    TeamScore[ETeamType.Red] = 0
    TeamScore[ETeamType.Blue] = 0
   
    for k,v in pairs(self.BattleResult.PlayerScore) do
        if v.TeamID == ETeamType.Blue or v.TeamID == ETeamType.Red then
            TeamScore[v.TeamID] = TeamScore[v.TeamID] + v.KillCount * self.KillScore
        end
    end
   
    self.BattleResult.TeamScore = TeamScore
    log_tree("UGCGameMode:OnRep_BattleResult", self.BattleResult)
   
    -- 发送游戏结束事件
    UGCEventSystem:SendEvent(CTCompetitioClientEvent.GameEndEvent, self.BattleResult)
end
```

```lua
            50.0
```

### 示例 5

```lua
function UGCPlayerController:ServerChangeTeam(NewTeamID, bForce)
    local GameMode = UGCGameSystem.GetGameMode()
   
    ugcprint("UGCPlayerController:ServerChangeTeam "..tostring(self.PlayerName)
        .." TeamID = "..tostring(self.TeamID).." NewTeamID = "..tostring(NewTeamID)
        .. "GameMode.GameState.CurrentGameStatus="..tostring(GameMode.GameState.CurrentGameStatus))
   
    if GameMode then
        -- 检查是否可以切换队伍
        if NewTeamID ~= self.TeamID and
           (GameMode.GameState.CurrentGameStatus == EGameStatus.Prepare or bForce) then
            ugcprint("UGCPlayerController:ServerChangeTeam ChangeTeamFinish.......1111")
            GameMode:PlayerChangeTeam(self.PlayerKey, NewTeamID)
        end
    else
        ugcprint("UGCPlayerController:ServerChangeTeam ChangeTeamFinish")
    end
end
```

### 示例 10

```lua
function UGCPlayerController:ServerChangeWeaponList(Index, SubIndex, NewID, NewCount)
    local GameMode = UGCGameSystem.GetGameMode()
   
    if GameMode and GameMode.GameState.CurrentGameStatus == EGameStatus.Prepare then
        local WeaponList = self.PlayerState.WeaponList
        local WeaponListNum = #WeaponList
       
        self.LastWeaponListIndex = Index
       
        -- 默认 ItemID 为 0（清空）
        if NewID == nil then
            NewID = 0
        end
       
        -- 修改现有武器
        if Index > 0 and Index <= WeaponListNum then
            ugcprint("WeaponListNum "..tostring(WeaponListNum))
            local WeaponConfig = WeaponList[Index]
            local SubItemListNum = #WeaponConfig.SubItemList
           
            -- 修改配件
            if SubIndex > 0 then
                ugcprint("SubItemListNum "..tostring(SubItemListNum))
               
                if SubIndex <= SubItemListNum then
                    -- 修改现有配件
                    ugcprint("SubItemListNum SubIndex <= SubItemListNum")
                    local SubItem = WeaponConfig.SubItemList[SubIndex]
                    SubItem.ItemID = NewID
                    if NewCount and NewCount > 0 then
                        SubItem.Count = NewCount
                    end
                else
                    -- 扩展配件列表
                    ugcprint("SubItemListNum SubIndex > SubItemListNum")
                    if NewCount and NewCount > 0 then
                        for i = SubItemListNum + 1, SubIndex do
                            if i == SubIndex then
                                -- 添加新配件
                                table.insert(WeaponConfig.SubItemList, {ItemID = NewID, Count = NewCount})
                            else
                                -- 添加占位符
                                table.insert(WeaponConfig.SubItemList, {ItemID = -1, Count = -1})
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            else
                -- 修改主武器
                WeaponConfig.ItemID = NewID
                if NewCount and NewCount > 0 then
                    WeaponConfig.Count = NewCount
                end
            end
           
            self.PlayerState:SetWeaponList(WeaponList)
           
        elseif Index > WeaponListNum then
            -- 添加新武器槽位
            ugcprint("WeaponListNum Index > WeaponListNum"..tostring(Index))
            WeaponList[Index] = {}
            WeaponList[Index].ItemID = NewID
            if NewCount and NewCount > 0 then
                WeaponList[Index].Count = NewCount
            end
            if 0 == SubIndex then
                WeaponList[Index].SubItemList = {}
            end
           
            self.PlayerState:SetWeaponList(WeaponList)
        end
    end
end
```

```lua
function IsWeaponInEquipRange(WeaponID, EquipRange)
    for _, Range in ipairs(EquipRange) do
        if WeaponID >= Range.MinID and WeaponID <= Range.MaxID then
            return true
        end
    end
    return false
end
```

```lua
local AliveTeamIDTab = {}
    local AllPlayerStates = TeamModeComponent:GetTeamates(-1)
    for i, PlayerStateWeakPtr in ipairs(AllPlayerStates) do
        local PlayerState = PlayerStateWeakPtr:Get()

        if PlayerState then
            print("Action_GameEndCheck:Execute AllPlayer PlayerName = "..PlayerState.PlayerName.." TeamID = "..PlayerState.TeamID)
        end

        if PlayerState and PlayerState:IsAlive() then
            local bCanInsert = true
            local NewTeam = PlayerState.TeamID
            for index, value in ipairs(AliveTeamIDTab) do
                if value == NewTeam then
                    bCanInsert = false
                end
            end
            if bCanInsert then
                print("Action_GameEndCheck:Execute NewTeam = "..NewTeam)
                table.insert(AliveTeamIDTab, NewTeam)
            end
        end
    end
```

### 示例 7

```lua
local TeamatesPlayerStates = {}
        local VictimState = ScriptGameplayStatics.GetPlayerStateByPlayerKey(self, self.VictimKey)
        if VictimState then
            TeamatesPlayerStates =TeamModeComponent:GetTeamates(VictimState.TeamID)
            print("Action_GameEndCheck:Execute Victim PlayerName = "..VictimState.PlayerName.." TeamID = "..VictimState.TeamID.." #TeamatesPlayerStates = "..#TeamatesPlayerStates)
        end

        --检查队友存活数量
        local bTeamatesAllDead = true
        for i, PlayerStateWeakPtr in ipairs(TeamatesPlayerStates) do
            local PlayerState = PlayerStateWeakPtr:Get()
            if PlayerState and PlayerState:IsAlive() then
                bTeamatesAllDead = false
            end
        end

        if bTeamatesAllDead then

            local GameResultData = {}
            GameResultData.bGameEnd = false
            GameResultData.bShowTeamatesInfo = true
            GameResultData.TeamRank = #AliveTeamIDTab + 1

            for i, PlayerStateWeakPtr in ipairs(TeamatesPlayerStates) do
                local PlayerState = PlayerStateWeakPtr:Get()
                if PlayerState then
                    PlayerState.TeamRank = #AliveTeamIDTab + 1
                    print("Action_GameEndCheck:Execute Teamates PlayerName = "..PlayerState.PlayerName.." PlayerState.TeamID = "..PlayerState.TeamID)

                    local PlayerController = ScriptGameplayStatics.GetPlayerControllerByPlayerKey(self, PlayerState.PlayerKey);
                    if PlayerController then
                        UnrealNetwork.CallUnrealRPC(PlayerController, PlayerController, "ClientRPC_SendEvent", UGCGameEventType.ShowSettlement, GameResultData)
                    end
                end
            end
        else
            --检查玩家数量
            local AliveNum = UGCGameSystem.GameState:GetAliveRealPlayerNum()
            VictimState.TeamRank = AliveNum + 1
            print("Action_GameEndCheck:Execute VictimState.TeamRank = "..VictimState.TeamRank);

            local GameResultData = {}
            GameResultData.bGameEnd = false
            GameResultData.bShowTeamatesInfo = false
            GameResultData.TeamRank = AliveNum + 1

            local VictimController = ScriptGameplayStatics.GetPlayerControllerByPlayerKey(self, VictimState.PlayerKey);
            if VictimController then
                UnrealNetwork.CallUnrealRPC(VictimController, VictimController, "ClientRPC_SendEvent", UGCGameEventType.ShowSettlement, GameResultData)
            end
        end
```

## 怪物

> 来源: 玩法案例及模板/俯视角生存模版 (id:20381)

### 示例 1

```lua
function BP_SpawnerManager:ApplyMonsterAttributes(Pawn)
    local MonsterConfig = TopDownSurvivor:GetMonsterConfig(Pawn.CharacterName)

    if MonsterConfig == nil then
        return
    end

    for AttributeType, Value in pairs(MonsterConfig.Attributes) do
        local GrowthType = GetGrowthTypeFromAttributeType(AttributeType)
        local GrowthValue = 1

        local CustomGrowth = MonsterConfig.CustomWaveGrowth[self.CurrentWaveIndex]
        if CustomGrowth and CustomGrowth[GrowthType] then
            GrowthValue = CustomGrowth[GrowthType]
        else
            local GrowthPerWave = MonsterConfig.GrowthPerWave[GrowthType] or 0
            GrowthValue = self.CurrentWaveIndex * GrowthPerWave
        end

        -- GameLog.Log("BP_SpawnerManager:ApplyMonsterAttributes", string.format("AttributeType: %s, GrowthValue: %f", AttributeType, GrowthValue))
        UGCAttributeSystem.SetGameAttributeValue(Pawn, AttributeType, Value + GrowthValue)
    end
end
```

## 技能

> 来源: 玩法案例及模板/俯视角生存模版 (id:20382)

### 示例 1

```lua
-- 将玩家角色始终朝向选择的目标
function Passive_AutoAim:SetControllerFaceTo()
    local Targets = self:GetSelectTargetActor(EPESkillSelectTarget.E_PESKILL_PickerType_AllTarget)
    -- GameLog.Log("AutoAim", string.format("Targets num: %d", #Targets))

    local Owner = self:GetOwnerActor()
    if Owner == nil or UGCPlayerPawnSystem.HasPawnState(Owner, EPawnState.Dead) then
        return
    end

    local PlayerController = UGCGameSystem.GetPlayerControllerByPlayerPawn(Owner)
    if PlayerController == nil then
        return
    end

    if Targets == nil or #Targets == 0 then
        return
    end

    for _, Target in ipairs(Targets) do
        local bTargetDead = UGCPersistEffectSystem.HasDynamicState(Target, "PawnState.Dead")

        if not bTargetDead then
            -- GameLog.Log("AutoAim", string.format("Face to target: %s", UGCObjectUtility.GetObjectName(Target)))

            local TargetLocation = Target:K2_GetActorLocation()
            local OwnerLocation = Owner:K2_GetActorLocation()
            local Direction = UGCMathUtility.SubtractVector(TargetLocation, OwnerLocation)
            Direction.Z = 0
            Direction = UGCMathUtility.Normal(Direction)
            local Rotation = UGCMathUtility.Conv_VectorToRotator(Direction)
            UGCPlayerControllerSystem.SetControlRotation(PlayerController, Rotation)
            return
        end
    end
end
```

### 示例 2

```lua
function Melee:OnEnableSkill_BP()
    Melee.SuperClass.OnEnableSkill_BP(self)

    local OwnerActor = self:GetOwnerActor()

    self.AttackRange = UGCAttributeSystem.GetGameAttributeValue(OwnerActor, E_CharacterAttributeType.AttackRange)
   
    self.AttackRangeChangeDelegate = UGCAttributeSystem.AddGameAttributeChangedDelegate(OwnerActor, E_CharacterAttributeType.AttackRange,
        function (Owner, AttrName, NewValue)
            self.AttackRange = UGCAttributeSystem.GetGameAttributeValue(OwnerActor, E_CharacterAttributeType.AttackRange)
        end
    )
end

function Melee:OnDisableSkill_BP()
    Melee.SuperClass.OnDisableSkill_BP(self)

    local OwnerActor = self:GetOwnerActor()

    UGCAttributeSystem.RemoveGameAttributeChangedDelegate(OwnerActor, E_CharacterAttributeType.AttackRange, self.AttackRangeChangeDelegate)
end
```

```lua
local TestCube = {};

TestCube.DynamicMaterial = nil



function TestCube:ReceiveBeginPlay()

    self.SuperClass.ReceiveBeginPlay(self);



    if not UGCGameSystem.IsServer() then

        --创建材质实例

        local MaterialInst = UE.LoadObject(UGCGameSystem.GetUGCResourcesFullPath('Asset/Blueprint/TestCubeMaterial.TestCubeMaterial'))

        self.DynamicMaterial = self.Cube:CreateAndSetMaterialInstanceDynamicFromMaterial(0, MaterialInst)



        self.Box.OnComponentBeginOverlap:Add(self.Box_OnComponentBeginOverlap,self)

    end

end



function TestCube:Box_OnComponentBeginOverlap(OverlappedComponent, OtherActor, OtherComp, OtherBodyIndex, bFromSweep, SweepResult)

    ugcprint("TestCube:Box_OnComponentBeginOverlap")

    --替换颜色为黄色

    self.DynamicMaterial:SetVectorParameterValue("Color_Modifier",{R = 1, G = 1, B = 0, A = 0})

end
```

## 弱网，断线重连处理

> 来源: 性能优化及异常处理 (id:270)

### 示例 1

```lua
--在此函数中返回的变量为属性同步变量
function MyMonster:GetReplicatedProperties()
    return "Health"
end

--OnRep_变量名为客户端受到变量同步后，响应的函数，此函数被调用时，变量已是同步后的值
function MyMonster:OnRep_Health(PrevHealth)
    print("MyMonster:OnRep_Health")
end
```

## 网络重连机制使用指南

> 来源: 性能优化及异常处理 (id:20348)

### 示例 1

```lua
-- 服务端进程终止后重连回调
function PlayerController:OnDSPlayerControllerReconnected()
    ugcprint("玩家进程终止后重连，完整初始化...")

    -- 重新初始化玩家状态
    self:ReInitializePlayerState()

    -- 重新发送必要数据给客户端
    self:SendInitialDataToClient()
end
```

### 示例 2

```lua
function PlayerController:OnDSPlayerControllerReconnected()
    -- 重建所有UI
    self:RebuildAllUI()

    -- 重新请求所有数据
    self:Server_RequestFullData()
end
```

### 示例 3

```lua
function PlayerController:ReceiveBeginPlay()
	self.SuperClass.ReceiveBeginPlay(self)

	if self:HasAuthority() then
    self.PlayerControllerLostDelegate:Add(self.OnDSPlayerControllerLost, self)
		self.PlayerControllerRecoveredDelegate:Add(self.OnDSPlayerControllerRecovered, self)
		self.PlayerControllerReconnectedDelegate:Add(self.OnDSPlayerControllerReconnected, self)
	else
		self.PlayerControllerReconnectedDelegate:Add(self.OnClientPlayerControllerReconnected, self)
		self.ClientPlayerControllerRecoveredDelegate:Add(self.OnClientPlayerControllerRecovered, self)
	end
end
```

### 示例 4

```lua
-- 使用值复制
-- 在 PlayerController 中定义 Replicated 变量
PlayerController.KillReward = 100  -- 会自动同步到重连的客户端
```

### 示例 5

```lua
-- 使用可靠 RPC + 客户端缓存
function PlayerController:Client_OnKillReward(Reward)
    self.ClientCachedKillReward = Reward  -- 本地缓存
    self:UpdateKillRewardUI()
end

-- 在重连时恢复缓存数据
function PlayerController:OnClientPlayerControllerRecovered()
    if self.ClientCachedKillReward then
        self:UpdateKillRewardUI()
    end
end
```

### 示例 6

```lua
-- 错误做法：用 RPC 传递重要状态
function MyGameMode:OnPlayerKilled(PlayerController, KillerController)
    -- 这样做的数据会丢失！
    PlayerController.Client_OnKillReward(100)
end
```

### 示例 7

```lua
function UGCPlayerController:ReceiveBeginPlay()
    UGCPlayerController.SuperClass.ReceiveBeginPlay(self)

    -- 服务端注册回调
    if self.Role == ENetRole.ROLE_Authority then
        -- 断线回调（包括静默断线和进程终止断线）
        self.PlayerControllerLostDelegate:Add(self.OnDSPlayerControllerLost, self)

        -- 静默重连回调
        self.PlayerControllerRecoveredDelegate:Add(self.OnDSPlayerControllerRecovered, self)

        -- 进程终止后重连回调
        self.PlayerControllerReconnectedDelegate:Add(self.OnDSPlayerControllerReconnected, self)
    else
        -- 客户端注册回调

        -- 静默重连回调
        self.ClientPlayerControllerRecoveredDelegate:Add(self.OnClientPlayerControllerRecovered, self)

        -- 进程终止后重连回调
        self.PlayerControllerReconnectedDelegate:Add(self.OnClientPlayerControllerReconnected, self)
    end
end

-- 服务端：打印重连类型
function PlayerController:OnDSPlayerControllerLost(PlayerController)
    ugcprint("玩家断线：PlayerControllerLostDelegate")
end

function PlayerController:OnDSPlayerControllerRecovered(PlayerController)
    ugcprint("静默重连成功：PlayerControllerRecoveredDelegate")
end

function PlayerController:OnDSPlayerControllerReconnected(PlayerController)
    ugcprint("进程终止后重连成功：PlayerControllerReconnectedDelegate")
end

-- 客户端：打印重连类型
function PlayerController:OnClientPlayerControllerRecovered()
    ugcprint("客户端静默重连：ClientPlayerControllerRecoveredDelegate")
    self:RefreshUI()
end

function PlayerController:OnDSPlayerControllerReconnected()
    ugcprint("客户端进程终止后重连：OnDSPlayerControllerReconnected")
    self:RebuildAllUI()
    self:Server_RequestFullData()
end
```

### 示例 8

```lua
-- 用 RPC 传递背包数据
function PlayerController:Client_UpdateBackpack(Items)
    self.BackpackItems = Items
end
```

### 示例 9

```lua
-- 使用值复制
PlayerController.BackpackItems = {}  -- Replicated 变量
-- 或者：使用可靠 RPC + 客户端缓存 + 重连时恢复
```

### 示例 10

```lua
-- 没有重连回调，UI不会刷新
function PlayerController:Client_UpdateHP(HP)
    self.HPText:SetText(HP)
end
```

### 示例 11

```lua
function PlayerController:Client_UpdateHP(HP)
    self.HPText:SetText(HP)
end

function PlayerController:OnClientPlayerControllerRecovered()
    -- 重连后刷新UI
    self:RefreshHPUI()
end
```

### 示例 12

```lua
function PlayerController:OnDSPlayerControllerReconnected()
    -- 只重建UI，没有重新请求数据
    self:RebuildAllUI()
end
```

### 示例 13

```lua
function PlayerController:OnDSPlayerControllerReconnected()
    -- 重建UI并重新请求数据
    self:RebuildAllUI()
    self:Server_RequestFullData()
end
```

### 示例 14

```lua
-- 用 UnReliable RPC 发送重要数据
function MyGameMode:OnPlayerKilled(Killer)
    PlayerController.Client_OnKillReward(100)  -- UnReliable
end
```

### 示例 15

```lua
-- 使用 Reliable RPC 或值复制
function MyGameMode:OnPlayerKilled(Killer)
    PlayerController.Client_OnKillReward(100)  -- Reliable
end

-- 或者使用值复制
PlayerController.KillReward = 100  -- Replicated
```

### 示例 16

```lua
-- 推荐：值复制
PlayerController.HP = 100  -- Replicated
PlayerController.MaxHP = 100  -- Replicated
PlayerController.Gold = 0  -- Replicated

-- 避免：RPC 传递状态
function PlayerController:Client_SetHP(HP)
    self.HP = HP
end
```

### 示例 17

```lua
-- 推荐：可靠 RPC + 本地缓存 + 重连恢复
function PlayerController:Client_OnItemAdded(ItemID)
    if not self.ClientCachedItems then
        self.ClientCachedItems = {}
    end
    table.insert(self.ClientCachedItems, ItemID)
    self:UpdateBackpackUI()
end

function PlayerController:OnClientPlayerControllerRecovered()
    if self.ClientCachedItems then
        self:UpdateBackpackUI()
    end
end
```

### 示例 18

```lua
function PlayerController:OnDSPlayerControllerLost(PlayerController)
    ugcprint("玩家断线：PlayerControllerLostDelegate")
end

function PlayerController:OnDSPlayerControllerRecovered(PlayerController)
    ugcprint("静默重连成功：PlayerControllerRecoveredDelegate")

    -- 静默重连，数据已由底层同步，只需处理业务逻辑
    self:SendClientWelcomeMessage()
end

function PlayerController:OnDSPlayerControllerReconnected(PlayerController)
    ugcprint("进程终止后重连成功：PlayerControllerReconnectedDelegate")

    -- 进程终止后重连，需要完整初始化
    self:ReInitializePlayerState()
    self:SendInitialDataToClient()
end
```

### 示例 19

```lua
-- 静默重连回调
function PlayerController:OnClientPlayerControllerRecovered()
    ugcprint("客户端静默重连：ClientPlayerControllerRecoveredDelegate")

    -- 刷新UI
    self:RefreshAllUI()

    -- 关闭断线提示
    if self.NetworkTipWidget then
        self.NetworkTipWidget:RemoveFromParent()
        self.NetworkTipWidget = nil
    end
end

-- 进程终止后重连回调
function PlayerController:OnDSPlayerControllerReconnected()
    ugcprint("客户端进程终止后重连：OnDSPlayerControllerReconnected")

    -- 重建UI
    self:RebuildAllUI()

    -- 重新请求数据
    self:Server_RequestFullData()
end

-- 数据同步示例
function PlayerController:Client_OnItemAdded(ItemID)
    -- 缓存背包数据
    if not self.ClientCachedBackpack then
        self.ClientCachedBackpack = {}
    end
    table.insert(self.ClientCachedBackpack, ItemID)

    -- 更新UI
    self:UpdateBackpackUI()
end

function PlayerController:RefreshAllUI()
    -- 刷新所有UI显示
    if self.HPText then self.HPText:SetText(self.HP) end
    if self.GoldText then self.GoldText:SetText(self.Gold) end
    if self.ClientCachedBackpack then
        self:UpdateBackpackUI()
    end
end
```
